# Trio versus default — quest review through the Hillsbrad/Arathi handoff

Reviewed 2026-10-13 from the current installed Forever route files. No guide files were changed.

## Scope and interpretation

- Trio: all 20 registered custom chapters, followed in their actual `#next` order. Blackfathom Deeps now runs at 25, before Wetlands First Loop, despite its older filename and XML position.
- Default: Dwarf/Gnome starters and applicable non-Hunter chapters through 29–32 Duskwood, including 21–23 Stonetalon/Ashenvale, both Wetlands alternatives, 27–29 Wetlands/Hillsbrad and 29–30 Ashenvale. Also includes the Menethil opening of 32–33 before arrival in Southshore. New Southshore/Arathi pickups in shared 32–33 are outside the omission count.
- Profiles: Dwarf Paladin, Gnome Warlock, and both Dwarf/Gnome Warrior alternatives. Race alternatives, optional professions/dungeons, quest-state conditions and alternative Wetlands chapters form a comparison envelope; the totals are not quests required of each character.
- A means an authored `.accept`; T means an authored `.turnin`. A turn-in is evidence of intended completion. `.complete` instructions and abandons are retained in the CSV ledger. Item starts, quest sharing and instant/repeatable exchanges can lack an explicit pickup tag.
- Numeric level selectors are retained as potentially applicable during leveling. Runtime level, XP-rate, profession, dungeon, prerequisite, item and quest-state gates are recorded, not simulated. Disabled `step << skip` actions and unrelated classes are excluded.

## Findings

**Coverage:** 440 default quest IDs and 460 trio IDs, with 544 IDs in their union. 350 have identical explicit A/T coverage; 5 gain a pickup or turn-in before handoff in trio; 104 are trio additions. 81 default quests with turn-ins are absent from active trio A/T instructions, plus two carry-only default pickups (1039 and 1059). Plea To The Alliance 634 is accepted but deferred. Further Instructions 1094 is only a conditional default recovery pickup.

**Already covered:** the Stalvan, Morbent Fel, Mor’Ladim, Eliza and Worgen in the Woods chains; A Noble Brew 335/336; Armed and Ready 325; Missing Diplomat through 1250 with 1264 held; James Hyal through 1301 with 1302 held; Sully’s Letter/Sara Balloo into A King’s Tribute 689; Cleansing the Eye 293; and the Gnomeregan main quests. Earlier reports describing these chains as absent are obsolete.

**Largest route omission:** the default 29–30 Ashenvale circuit has 22 missing turn-in IDs and two missing outgoing breadcrumbs. This is a substantial geographic route difference. Stonetalon has further missing chains; inserting all of them would materially expand trio’s itinerary.

**Warlock progression:** Devourer of Souls 1716, Heartswood 1738, The Binding 1739, What Is Love? 65602 and The Binding 65603 have no trio actions. Default explicitly routes the Succubus/Incubus summons. These include alternative chains, rather than five universally required quests. You Have Served Us Well 397 is also absent. Trio’s Voidwalker chain is present, and level-30 referrals 1798/4738 are picked up for later default travel.

**Small referral gap:** Tinkmaster Overspark 2923 is absent, although Save Techbot’s Brain 2922 is already accepted and delivered. This is a missing referral, not missing Techbot quest coverage.

**Other omissions:** starter mail/class scrolls, several Dun Morogh/Loch additions, Bashal’Aran/Buzzboxes, Redridge fish/murloc quests, early Stranglethorn hunts, cooking and optional Wailing Caverns quests. Apprentice’s Duties 471 and Harvesting the Harvesters 92909 are deliberately disabled in trio. These omissions are not all broken prerequisites.

**Previous unresolved pickups:** A New Threat 170 and The State of the Mines 92745 are no longer accepted. Unrequited Love 98461 is delivered during Wetlands First Loop. A Friend of the Family 98189 → 98190 → 98191 has pickup and delivery actions through the post-Stockades city visit. Bloom of the Heavens 98208 is picked up and delivered, with 98209 held.

## Handoff and carried quests

**Plea To The Alliance 634 needs a manual delivery at the first Refuge Pointe visit.** Default delivered it in 27–29 and does not turn it in in 32–33. Trio carries it and mentions its destination, but there is no delivery objective in the shared chapter. Default also assumes the Refuge Pointe flight path; trio never obtains it. The previously documented plan is to run the first Southshore-to-Refuge trip and unlock the flight path there.

[Trio pickup/hold](<C:/Program Files (x86)/World of Warcraft/_classic_beta_/Interface/AddOns/RXPGuides/Guides/Forever/Custom-Forever-30-Wetlands-Final-Loop.lua:287>); [Default flight and Captain Nials visit](<C:/Program Files (x86)/World of Warcraft/_classic_beta_/Interface/AddOns/RXPGuides/Guides/Forever/GuideVault_Filtered/RestedXP-Forever-Guide-A__32-33-Hillsbrad-Arathi.lua:469>).

**Bloom of the Heavens 98209 has no authored turn-in anywhere in the filtered default.** Trio explicitly tells the party to deliver it to Thundris Windweaver at the default 35–37 Auberdine boat stop. It is a manual continuation reminder, not an automatically tracked default delivery.

[Bloom handoff reminder](<C:/Program Files (x86)/World of Warcraft/_classic_beta_/Interface/AddOns/RXPGuides/Guides/Forever/Custom-Forever-30-32-Gnomeregan-City-Deliveries.lua:471>).

**Fourteen pickup-only quests are carried:** 231, 538, 634, 689, 690, 1179, 1264, 1302, 1718, 1798, 2904, 4738, 95795 and 98209. Gnome Improvement 2948 also may remain carried when Silver Bar/Moss Agate are missing; it has a conditional turn-in, so is not part of that pickup-only count.

**Shared entry is otherwise prepared:** trio handles the Menethil Missing Diplomat/James Hyal exchanges before Southshore. Default skips those opening steps in Hillsbrad; Third Fleet has no zone skip but is already delivered by trio. Southshore 538 is conditional on An Old History Book; 689/690 and the later class/Brassbolts referrals are present. Local Hillsbrad/Arathi quests have their own default pickups.

**Later Ratchet travel still needs the existing reminder:** default’s Warlock flight assumes an earlier Ratchet unlock. Trio tells the Warlock to travel with the party and obtain it on first arrival.

## Validation and limits

- Followed all 20 custom `#next` links; all XML-listed trio chapters are reachable and the last link targets default 32–33 Hillsbrad/Arathi.
- Checked every extracted pickup, objective, turn-in and abandon in scope. Found no quest with only completion objectives and neither pickup nor delivery in trio. Found no default-versus-trio turn-in mismatch hidden behind race/class selectors for IDs both routes deliver across the four selected profiles.
- Turn-ins without explicit pickups are 184 (item-start deed, gated on being in the log), 308 (Jarven’s immediate exchange) and 91736 (three Applejack exchanges with a three-bottle collection objective). These are not automatically classified as unresolved pickups. The earlier conditional 337 delivery uses the history-book item; the explicit later pickup/turn-in is a recovery path.
- Confirmed parser behavior: `GuideLoader.lua:36` handles slash OR, space AND, negation and level selectors; `functions.lua:1350` normalizes negative turn-in IDs and makes them skip when missing, with additional incomplete skipping when a positive reward argument is supplied. State gates mean A/T presence does not guarantee execution.
- Default vault files are local extracted research sources, not loaded directly by the XML. The runtime handoff requires the original default guide to be registered. No in-game reload, quest availability, spawn, reward or character-state validation was available.
- No external quest database research was used for this source-to-source comparison. Destinations described above follow installed instructions and existing handoff notes. No guide files were edited.
